## Installation

```bash
npm i @steempro/sds
```

```ts
import { SDS } from '@steempro/sds';       // ESM / TypeScript
const { SDS } = require('@steempro/sds');  // CommonJS
```

Requires Node 18+ (or Deno / Bun / a browser — anything with `fetch`), or pass your own `{ fetch }`.

## Initialization & instances

| Option | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `instance` | `"sds0"` | Bundled name (`sds`, `sds0`, `sds1`), a registered name, or a full URL |
| `baseUrl` | – | Full base URL (takes precedence over `instance`) |
| `instances` | `{}` | Extra `{ name: url }` pairs for this client only |
| `timeout` | `30000` | Request timeout in ms (`0` disables it) |
| `retries` | `2` | Retries for network / timeout / 5xx / 429 failures |
| `retryDelay` | `300` | Base delay of the exponential backoff in ms |
| `headers` | `{}` | Extra headers sent with every request |
| `fetch` | `globalThis.fetch` | Custom fetch implementation |
| `validate` | `true` | Client side parameter validation (type / range / allow list) |
| `encode` | `true` | URL-encode path segments |
| `onRequest` / `onResponse` | – | Logging / tracing hooks |

```ts
import { SDS, registerInstance, listInstances, DEFAULT_SDS_INSTANCES } from '@steempro/sds';

const sds0 = new SDS();                                  // https://sds0.steemworld.org
const sds1 = new SDS({ instance: 'sds1' });              // https://sds1.steemworld.org
const custom = new SDS({ baseUrl: 'https://my-sds.example.org' });

// Your own instance — per client …
const sds9 = new SDS({ instance: 'sds9', instances: { sds9: 'https://sds9.example.org' } });
// … or process wide:
registerInstance('sds9', 'https://sds9.example.org');
const again = new SDS({ instance: 'sds9' });

console.log(DEFAULT_SDS_INSTANCES, listInstances());
```

## Calling methods

Every method supports **positional (route order)** arguments, a **single object** keyed by
parameter name, and an optional trailing **node-style callback**:

```ts
// 1. Promise, positional (order = route order)
await sds.chain.getAccountNamesByPrefix('ste', 50);

// 2. Promise, object style
await sds.chain.getAccountNamesByPrefix({ prefix: 'ste', limit: 50 });

// 3. Callback (either style) — (error, result) => void
sds.chain.getAccountNamesByPrefix('ste', 50, (error, names) => {
  if (error) return console.error(error.code, error.message);
  console.log(names);
});

// 4. Generic entry point (works for methods missing from the bundle too)
await sds.call('chain_api', 'getAccountNames', [10, 0], { timeout: 5000 });

// 5. Raw GET, envelope unwrapped for you
await sds.request('/chain_api/getConfig');
```

Rules the argument resolver follows:

- Trailing optional parameters are **omitted** — SDS applies their documented defaults.
- Skipped middle parameters are filled with the documented default, or the literal
  `null` placeholder SDS accepts (e.g. `observer`).
- Routes with two parameters in one segment become `from-to` (e.g. `getBlocksInRange(1, 5)` → `/1-5`).
- CSV parameters accept an array or a comma separated string and are joined with `,`.
- Community parameters take a **hive community id** — always `hive-` prefixed
  (e.g. `hive-160125`). Bare ids, titles and regular accounts are rejected up front
  (SDS would silently answer with empty data); disable with `validate: false`.
- Timestamp parameters (`fromTime`, `toTime`, `blockTime`, `…date`) accept **any date
  form** — a `Date`, `"2020-09-13"`, `"2020-09-13 12:30:00"`, `"2020/09/13"`,
  `"2020-09-13T12:30:00+02:00"` or unix seconds — and are converted to unix **seconds**
  (the unit SDS expects) before the request is sent; zone-less strings are treated as
  UTC and 12+ digit numbers are read as milliseconds. Values that cannot be parsed
  throw an `SDSValidationError` listing the accepted formats instead of silently
  returning empty or "now" data.
- Client side validation (`validate: true`) rejects bad types, out-of-range values and
  values outside an allow list before anything is sent.

## Mapping responses with `mapSds`

Many SDS list/search methods return a **column oriented table** instead of objects:

```json
{
  "cols": { "link_id": 0, "author": 6, "permlink": 7 },
  "rows": [[114136936, 0, 69.305, 0, "", "", "alice", "hello"]]
}
```

`mapSds()` converts that into plain row objects — and passes every other payload
(plain JSON, arrays, raw `{ code, result }` envelopes) through unchanged:

```ts
import { mapSds, isSDSTable } from '@steempro/sds';

const feed = await sds.feeds.getActivePostsByCreated({ limit: 1 });
const rows = mapSds(feed);
// [ { link_id: 114136936, author: 'alice', permlink: 'hello', … }, … ]

// …or straight from the promise:
const rows2 = await sds.feeds
  .getActivePostsByCreated({ limit: 1 })
  .then(mapSds);

// Narrow first if the payload type is unknown:
const payload: unknown = await sds.request('/feeds_api/getActivePostsByTrending');
const rows3 = isSDSTable(payload) ? mapSds(payload) : payload;
```

Implementation:

```ts
export function mapSds(data: SDSTable): SDSRow[];
export function mapSds<T = any>(data: unknown): T;

export function mapSds(data: unknown): unknown {
  const payload = unwrapEnvelope(data);          // { code, result } → result
  if (!isSDSTable(payload)) return payload;      // not a table → unchanged

  const cols = payload.cols;                     // { name: index } or [name, …]
  const rows = payload.rows ?? [];
  const names = Array.isArray(cols) ? cols : Object.keys(cols);

  return rows.map((row) => {
    if (Array.isArray(row)) {
      const out = {};
      names.forEach((name, position) => {
        out[name] = row[Array.isArray(cols) ? position : cols[name]];
      });
      return out;                               // positional row → keyed object
    }
    if (row && typeof row === "object") return { ...row };
    return { [names[0]]: row };                 // scalar row of a 1-column table
  });
}
```

Why this version differs from the naive `Object.keys(cols).map((k, i) => …)` version:

- **Index lookup, not position**: values are taken at `cols[name]`, so an unordered
  `cols` map (or a subset of columns) maps correctly.
- **Array `cols`** (`["a", "b"]`) is supported as well as the object form.
- **Already keyed rows** are shallow-copied instead of being zipped against `cols`.
- **Scalar rows** of a single-column table become `{ name: value }` instead of `undefined`.
- **Empty table** (`cols` without `rows`) yields `[]`, never `undefined`.
- **Raw envelopes** (`{ code: 0, result: { cols, rows } }` from `sds.request()` of a
  non-unwrapped payload) are unwrapped first; error envelopes pass through.
- **Typed overloads**: a known `SDSTable` input gives `SDSRow[]`, anything else keeps
  its own type (`mapSds<T>(x)`), so plain JSON is never lied about as rows.

## Error handling

Every failure is an `SDSError` subclass with a stable `code` plus `module`, `method`, `url`:

| Class | `code` | When |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `SDSValidationError` | `ERR_SDS_VALIDATION` | Parameter failed type / range / allow-list validation |
| `SDSArgumentError` | `ERR_SDS_ARGUMENT` | Unknown method, instance or parameter; wrong arity |
| `SDSNetworkError` | `ERR_SDS_NETWORK` | Request never reached SDS (DNS, CORS, offline) |
| `SDSTimeoutError` | `ERR_SDS_TIMEOUT` | Request exceeded `timeout` (`error.timeout`) |
| `SDSAbortError` | `ERR_SDS_ABORT` | Cancelled through an `AbortSignal` |
| `SDSHttpError` | `ERR_SDS_HTTP` | Non-success HTTP status (`error.status`, `error.body`) |
| `SDSResponseError` | `ERR_SDS_RESPONSE` | HTTP 200 but not the expected JSON envelope |
| `SDSApiError` | `ERR_SDS_API` | Application error `{ code: -1, error }` (`error.apiCode`) |

```ts
import { SDSApiError, isSDSError } from '@steempro/sds';

try {
  await sds.posts.getPost('alice', 'missing');
} catch (error) {
  if (error instanceof SDSApiError) console.log(error.apiCode, error.errorMessage);
  if (isSDSError(error)) console.log(error.toJSON());  // log / serialize safely
}

// Callback style delivers the same errors instead of throwing:
sds.posts.getPost('alice', 'missing', (error, post) => {
  if (error) return console.error(error.code, error.message);
  console.log(post);
});
```

Retries: network failures, timeouts, HTTP 5xx and 429 are retried with exponential
backoff (`retries`, `retryDelay`, overridable per call). Application errors are never retried.

## Introspection

```ts
sds.listModules();                                 // all modules
sds.listMethods('chain_api');                      // methods of one module
sds.describeMethod('chain_api.getAccountNames');   // path, params, defaults, result
sds.hasMethod('posts_api', 'getPost');            // true
sds.module('chain_api');                          // → sds.chain namespace
sds.referenceVersion;                             // bundled reference version

import { listModules, listMethods, findMethod, API_REFERENCE } from '@steempro/sds';
```

## API reference

Each entry shows the documentation, the route, the parameters, the generated TypeScript
implementation (overloads) and runnable examples (promise, object style, callback, generic `call`).

---

### 1. `blocks_api`

Namespace: `sds.blocks` · Group: Base · 14 methods

#### 1.1 `sds.blocks.getConfig`

Global index: **#1** · Anchor: [`#11-sdsblocksgetconfig`](#11-sdsblocksgetconfig)

Returns this module's active configuration.

- Route: `/blocks_api/getConfig`
- Result: JSON Object
- Reference example: `/blocks_api/getConfig`
- Example request: `https://sds0.steemworld.org/blocks_api/getConfig`

**Parameters**

_No parameters._

**Implementation**

```ts
getConfig<T = SDSJsonObject>(callback: Callback<T>): Promise<void>;
getConfig<T = SDSJsonObject>(): Promise<T>;
```

**Examples**

```ts
const result = await sds.blocks.getConfig();
sds.blocks.getConfig((error, result) => {
  if (error) return console.error(error.code, error.message);
  console.log(result);
});

await sds.call('blocks_api', 'getConfig');
```

#### 1.2 `sds.blocks.getBlock`

Global index: **#2** · Anchor: [`#12-sdsblocksgetblock`](#12-sdsblocksgetblock)

Returns the block for the given :blockNum.

- Route: `/blocks_api/getBlock/:blockNum/:withTransactions?/:withVirtualOps?`
- Result: JSON Object
- Section: Blocks
- Reference example: `/blocks_api/getBlock/50000001`
- Example request: `https://sds0.steemworld.org/blocks_api/getBlock/10/true/true`

**Parameters**

| # | Name | Type | Required | Default | Range | Allowed values |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `blockNum` | int | yes | — | 1 – 110139661 | — |
| 2 | `withTransactions` | bool | no | `true` | — | true | false | 1 | 0 |
| 3 | `withVirtualOps` | bool | no | `true` | — | true | false | 1 | 0 |

**Implementation**

```ts
interface BlocksGetBlockArgs {
  blockNum: number;  // int · 1…110139661
  withTransactions?: boolean;  // bool · default `true` · one of: true | false | 1 | 0
  withVirtualOps?: boolean;  // bool · default `true` · one of: true | false | 1 | 0
}

getBlock<T = SDSJsonObject>(args: BlocksGetBlockArgs, callback: Callback<T>): Promise<void>;
getBlock<T = SDSJsonObject>(args: BlocksGetBlockArgs): Promise<T>;
getBlock<T = SDSJsonObject>(blockNum: number, withTransactions: boolean | undefined, withVirtualOps: boolean | undefined, callback: Callback<T>): Promise<void>;
getBlock<T = SDSJsonObject>(blockNum: number, withTransactions?: boolean, withVirtualOps?: boolean): Promise<T>;
```

**Examples**

```ts
const result = await sds.blocks.getBlock(10, true, true);
// …or object style:
// const result = await sds.blocks.getBlock({ blockNum: 10, withTransactions: true, withVirtualOps: true });
sds.blocks.getBlock(10, true, true, (error, result) => {
  if (error) return console.error(error.code, error.message);
  console.log(result);
});

await sds.call('blocks_api', 'getBlock', [10, true, true]);
```

#### 1.3 `sds.blocks.getBlockById`

Global index: **#3** · Anchor: [`#13-sdsblocksgetblockbyid`](#13-sdsblocksgetblockbyid)

Returns the block for the given :blockId.

- Route: `/blocks_api/getBlockById/:blockId/:withTransactions?/:withVirtualOps?`
- Result: JSON Object
- Section: Blocks
- Reference example: `/blocks_api/getBlockById/02faf0817156fbe4752dd8de2b22b7a52dd37e2d`
- Example request: `https://sds0.steemworld.org/blocks_api/getBlockById/0000000000000000000000000000000000000000/true/true`

**Parameters**

| # | Name | Type | Required | Default | Range | Allowed values |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `blockId` | hex_string (40) | yes | — | — | — |
| 2 | `withTransactions` | bool | no | `true` | — | true | false | 1 | 0 |
| 3 | `withVirtualOps` | bool | no | `true` | — | true | false | 1 | 0 |

**Implementation**

```ts
interface BlocksGetBlockByIdArgs {
  blockId: string;  // hex_string (40)
  withTransactions?: boolean;  // bool · default `true` · one of: true | false | 1 | 0
  withVirtualOps?: boolean;  // bool · default `true` · one of: true | false | 1 | 0
}

getBlockById<T = SDSJsonObject>(args: BlocksGetBlockByIdArgs, callback: Callback<T>): Promise<void>;
```

#### 11.2 `sds.notifications.getFilteredUnreadCount`

Global index: **#93** · Anchor: [`#112-sdsnotificationsgetfilteredunreadcount`](#112-sdsnotificationsgetfilteredunreadcount)

Returns the number of new (unread) notifications for the given :account and :filter. Filters can be set for all notification types at once with ':filter.default' or for each type individually. For example, if you want to use specific filters for vote notifications, you can define them with ':filter.vote'. The vote amount filter uses absolute values. So, when setting 'minVoteAmount' to '0.1', the results will also include downvotes with an amount equal to or lower than '-0.1'.

- Route: `/notifications_api/getFilteredUnreadCount/:account/:filter`
- Result: Integer
- Section: Filtered
- Reference example: `/notifications_api/getFilteredUnreadCount/steemchiller/{"default":{"minReputation":25},"mention":{"minSP":1000,"minReputation":50},"vote":{"minVoteAmount":0.1}}`
- Example request: `https://sds0.steemworld.org/notifications_api/getFilteredUnreadCount/alice/%7B%22default%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22follow%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22mention%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22reply%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22resteem%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22vote%22%3A%7B%7D%7D`

**Parameters**

| # | Name | Type | Required | Default | Range | Allowed values |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `account` | account_name | yes | — | — | — |
| 2 | `filter` | json_object | yes | — | — | — |

**Implementation**

```ts
interface NotificationsGetFilteredUnreadCountArgs {
  account: string;  // account_name
  filter: SDSJsonObject | string;  // json_object · 6 documented fields
}

getFilteredUnreadCount<T = number>(args: NotificationsGetFilteredUnreadCountArgs, callback: Callback<T>): Promise<void>;
getFilteredUnreadCount<T = number>(args: NotificationsGetFilteredUnreadCountArgs): Promise<T>;
getFilteredUnreadCount<T = number>(account: string, filter: SDSJsonObject | string, callback: Callback<T>): Promise<void>;
getFilteredUnreadCount<T = number>(account: string, filter: SDSJsonObject | string): Promise<T>;
```

**Examples**

```ts

// …or object style:
// const result = await sds.notifications.getFilteredUnreadCount({ account: 'alice', filter: { "default": {}, follow: {}, mention: {}, reply: {}, resteem: {}, vote: {} } });
```

#### 11.3 `sds.notifications.getFilteredUnreadSummary`

Global index: **#94** · Anchor: [`#113-sdsnotificationsgetfilteredunreadsummary`](#113-sdsnotificationsgetfilteredunreadsummary)

Filters can be set for all notification types at once with ':filter.default' or for each type individually. #### 11.4 `sds.notifications.getFilteredNotificationsByStatus`

Global index: **#95** · Anchor: [`#114-sdsnotificationsgetfilterednotificationsbystatus`](#114-sdsnotificationsgetfilterednotificationsbystatus)

Returns :status notifications for the given :account and :filter ordered by time descending. Filters can be set for all notification types at once with ':filter.default' or for each type individually. For example, if you want to use specific filters for vote notifications, you can define them with ':filter.vote'. The vote amount filter uses absolute values. So, when setting 'minVoteAmount' to '0.1', the results will also include downvotes with an amount equal to or lower than '-0.1'.

- Section: Filtered

#### 11.5 `sds.notifications.getFilteredNotificationsByStatusType`

Global index: **#96** · Anchor: [`#115-sdsnotificationsgetfilterednotificationsbystatustype`](#115-sdsnotificationsgetfilterednotificationsbystatustype)

Returns :status notifications for the given :account, :type, and :filter ordered by time descending. Filters can be set for all notification types at once with ':filter.default' or for each type individually. For example, if you want to use specific filters for vote notifications, you can define them with ':filter.vote'. The vote amount filter uses absolute values. So, when setting 'minVoteAmount' to '0.1', the results will also include downvotes with an amount equal to or lower than '-0.1'.

- Route: `/notifications_api/getFilteredNotificationsByStatusType/:account/:status/:type/:filter/:limit?/:offset?`
- Result: JSON Object
- Max. limit: 2500
- Section: Filtered
- Reference example: `/notifications_api/getFilteredNotificationsByStatusType/steemchiller/new/follow/{"default":{"minSP":1000,"minReputation":50}}`
- Example request: `https://sds0.steemworld.org/notifications_api/getFilteredNotificationsByStatusType/alice/all/vote/%7B%22default%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22follow%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22mention%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22reply%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22resteem%22%3A%7B%7D%2C%22vote%22%3A%7B%7D%7D/250/0`

**Parameters**

| # | Name | Type | Required | Default | Range | Allowed values |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `account` | account_name | yes | — | — | — |
| 2 | `status` | fixed_value | yes | — | — | all | new | old |
| 3 | `type` | fixed_value | yes | — | — | vote | reply | follow | mention | resteem |
| 4 | `filter` | json_object | yes | — | — | — |
| 5 | `limit` | int | no | `250` | 1 – 2500 | — |
| 6 | `offset` | int | no | `0` | ≥ 0 | — |

**Implementation**

```ts

  account: string;  // account_name

  filter: SDSJsonObject | string;  // json_object · 6 documented fields

  offset?: number;  // int · default `0` · min 0
}
```
